---------------- units ---------------------------------------------------\n   187→        units = nwb.units\n   188→        classification = np.asarray(units['classification'][:])\n   189→        good = np.flatnonzero(classification == 'good')\n   190→        if len(good) == 0:\n   191→            io.close()\n   192→            return None\n   193→\n   194→        anno = np.asarray(units['anno_name'][:])[good]\n   195→        egroups = np.asarray(units['electrode_group'].data[:])[good]\n   196→        probe_target = np.array([json.loads(g.location)['brain_regions'] for g in egroups])\n   197→\n   198→        # unit -> electrode -> CCF x, for the hemisphere rule of the reference code\n   199→        er = units['electrodes']\n   200→        cum = np.asarray(er.data[:])\n   201→        elec_idx = np.asarray(er.target.data[:])[cum - 1][good]\n   202→        elec_x = np.asarray(nwb.electrodes['x'][:], dtype=np.float64)[elec_idx]\n   203→        hemisphere = np.where(elec_x >= ML_MIDLINE_UM, 'left', 'right')\n   204→        # fall back on the insertion's targeted hemisphere where CCF x is missing\n   205→        missing = np.isnan(elec_x)\n   206→        if missing.any():\n   207→            hemisphere[missing] = np.array([t.split(' ')[0] for t in probe_target])[missing]\n   208→\n   209→        region_name = np.array([annotation_to_region(a, t) for a, t in zip(anno, probe_target)])\n   210→        unknown = region_name == None  # noqa: E711\n   211→        if unknown.any():\n   212→            print(f'  WARNING {session_id}: {unknown.sum()} units with unmapped annotation '\n   213→                  f'{set(anno[unknown])}', flush=True)\n   214→            region_name[unknown] = 'OtherCortex'\n   215→        region_idx = np.array([REGIONS.index(r) for r in region_name], dtype=np.int64)\n   216→\n   217→        # ---------------- trial curation -----------------------------------------\n   218→        # In 9 sessions the ephys recording covers only a subset of the behavioural\n   219→        # trials, so the observed trials have to be located in the trials table first.\n   220→        # units.obs_intervals is identical for every unit of a session and its rows\n   221→        # match the [start_time, stop_time] of the observed trials exactly (verified\n   222→        # for all 174 files).\n   223→        obs = np.asarray(units['obs_intervals'][int(good[0])], dtype=np.float64)\n   224→        obs_trial = np.searchsorted(trial_start, obs[:, 0] - 1e-6)\n   225→        if not np.allclose(trial_start[obs_trial], obs[:, 0]):\n   226→            raise RuntimeError(f'{session_id}: obs_intervals do not match the trials table')\n   227→\n   228→        # (a) reference `get_regular_trial_mask`: no auto-water, no free-water trials\n   229→        keep = (~auto_water[obs_trial]) & (~free_water[obs_trial])\n   230→        # (b) recording-stability annotation: keep only trials annotated 'good' for\n   231→        #     every retained unit (i.e. for every probe insertion contributing units)\n   232→        is_good_trials = np.asarray(units['is_good_trials'][:])[good]   # (n_good, n_obs)\n   233→        keep &= is_good_trials.all(axis=0)\n   234→        keep_idx = obs_trial[keep]\n   235→        n_trials = len(keep_idx)\n   236→        n_trials_obs = len(obs_trial)\n   237→        if n_trials < 2:\n   238→            io.close()\n   239→            return None\n   240→\n   241→        go = go_times[keep_idx]\n   242→        timing['setup'] = time.time() - t0\n   243→\n   244→        # ---------------- neural ---------------------------------------------------\n   245→        t1 = time.time()\n   246→        sv = units['spike_times']\n   247→        ends = np.asarray(sv.data[:])\n   248→        starts = np.concatenate([[0], ends[:-1]])\n   249→        flat_spikes = np.asarray(sv.target.data[:], dtype=np.float64)\n   250→\n   251→        n_neurons = len(good)\n   252→        fr = np.empty((n_neurons, n_trials, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n   253→        for i, u in enumerate(good):\n   254→            st = flat_spikes[starts[u]:ends[u]]\n   255→            fr[i] = bin_spikes_rate(st, go)\n   256→\n   257→        # A trial in which not one of the (hundreds of) good units fired a single spike\n   258→        # means the amplifiers were not running: the recording can stop part-way through\n   259→        # the last annotated trial. Such trials carry no neural information at all.\n   260→        nonempty = fr.sum(axis=(0, 2)) > 0\n   261→        if not nonempty.all():\n   262→            n_empty = int((~nonempty).sum())\n   263→            print(f'  {session_id}: dropping {n_empty} trial(s) with no spikes at all',\n   264→                  flush=True)\n   265→            fr = fr[:, nonempty, :]\n   266→            keep_idx = keep_idx[nonempty]\n   267→            go = go[nonempty]\n   268→            n_trials = len(keep_idx)\n   269→            if n_trials < 2:\n   270→                io.close()\n   271→                return None\n   272→        timing['neural'] = time.time() - t1\n   273→\n   274→        # ---------------- inputs ---------------------------------------------------\n   275→        t1 = time.time()\n   276→        # tone onset := last sample-epoch start before the go cue\n   277→        sample_start = _events(nwb, 'sample_start_times')\n   278→        tone_pos = np.searchsorted(sample_start, go, side='right') - 1\n   279→        if np.any(tone_pos < 0):\n   280→            raise RuntimeError(f'{session_id}: trial without a preceding sample epoch')\n   281→        tone_time = sample_start[tone_pos]\n   282→        # time from tone onset at each bin centre (seconds, continuous)\n   283→        time_from_tone = BIN_CENTERS[None, :] + (go - tone_time)[:, None]   # (n_trials, NBINS)\n   284→\n   285→        photostim_on = np.zeros((n_trials, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n   286→        try:\n   287→            ps_start = _events(nwb, 'photostim_start_times')\n   288→            ps_stop = _events(nwb, 'photostim_stop_times')\n   289→        except KeyError:\n   290→            ps_start = ps_stop = np.zeros(0)\n   291→        if len(ps_start) and len(ps_start) == len(ps_stop):\n   292→            for j, g in enumerate(go):\n   293→                m = (ps_stop > g + OFF_START) & (ps_start < g + OFF_END)\n   294→                if m.any():\n   295→                    photostim_on[j] = interval_overlap_bins(ps_start[m], ps_stop[m], g)\n   296→        elif len(ps_start) != len(ps_stop):\n   297→            print(f'  WARNING {session_id}: photostim start/stop counts differ '\n   298→                  f'({len(ps_start)}/{len(ps_stop)}); photostim input left at 0', flush=True)\n   299→        timing['input'] = time.time() - t1\n   300→\n   301→        # ---------------- outputs --------------------------------------------------\n   302→        t1 = time.time()\n   303→        instr_k = instruction[keep_idx]\n   304→        out_k = outcome[keep_idx]\n   305→        early_k = early_lick[keep_idx]\n   306→\n   307→        # choice: hit -> instructed side, miss -> opposite side, ignore -> no lick\n   308→        choice = np.full(n_trials, CHOICE_NOLICK, dtype=np.int64)\n   309→        hit = out_k == 'hit'\n   310→        miss = out_k == 'miss'\n   311→        choice[hit & (instr_k == 'left')] = CHOICE_LEFT\n   312→        choice[hit & (instr_k == 'right')] = CHOICE_RIGHT\n   313→        choice[miss & (instr_k == 'left')] = CHOICE_RIGHT\n   314→        choice[miss & (instr_k == 'right')] = CHOICE_LEFT\n   315→\n   316→        outcome_code = np.array([OUTCOME_CODE[o] for o in out_k], dtype=np.int64)\n   317→        early_code = (early_k == 'early').astype(np.int64)\n   318→\n   319→        tongue_y_binned, tongue_visible_frac = bin_tongue(nwb, trial_start, go, keep_idx)\n   320→        tongue_class, pct = discretise_tongue(tongue_y_binned)\n   321→        timing['output'] = time.time() - t1\n   322→\n   323→        io.close()\n   324→\n   325→    # ---------------- assemble ----------------------------------------------------\n   326→    neural = [fr[:, j, :] for j in range(n_trials)]\n   327→    inputs = [np.stack([time_from_tone[j], photostim_on[j]]).astype(np.float32)\n   328→              for j in range(n_trials)]\n   329→    outputs = [np.stack([np.full(NBINS, choice[j]),\n   330→                         np.full(NBINS, outcome_code[j]),\n   331→                         np.full(NBINS, early_code[j]),\n   332→                         tongue_class[j]]).astype(np.int64)\n   333→               for j in range(n_trials)]\n   334→\n   335→    result = dict(\n   336→        session_id=session_id,\n   337→        subject=subject,\n   338→        file=os.path.basename(path),\n   339→        neural=neural,\n   340→        input=inputs,\n   341→        output=outputs,\n   342→        brain_region_idx=region_idx,\n   343→        hemisphere=np.where(hemisphere == 'left', 0, 1).astype(np.int8),\n   344→        n_trials_raw=n_trials_raw,\n   345→        n_trials_observed=n_trials_obs,\n   346→        n_trials=n_trials,\n   347→        n_neurons=n_neurons,\n   348→        trial_index=keep_idx,                     # index into the raw trials table\n   349→        tongue_percentiles=pct,\n   350→        tongue_visible_frac=float(tongue_visible_frac),\n   351→        timing=timing,\n   352→        total_time=time.time() - t0,\n   353→    )\n   354→    if collect_debug:\n   355→        result['debug'] = dict(\n   356→            go=go, tone_time=tone_time, trial_start=trial_start[keep_idx],\n   357→            trial_stop=trial_stop[keep_idx], time_from_tone=time_from_tone,\n   358→            photostim_on=photostim_on, tongue_y=tongue_y_binned, tongue_class=tongue_class,\n   359→            fr=fr, instruction=instr_k, outcome=out_k, early=early_k,\n   360→            choice=choice, anno=anno, region_name=region_name, hemisphere=hemisphere,\n   361→        )\n   362→    return result\n   363→\n   364→\n   365→def bin_tongue(nwb, trial_start, go, keep_idx):\n   366→    \"\"\"Mean tongue y-position per 50 ms bin, NaN where the tongue is not visible.\n   367→\n   368→    Video frames are first assigned to the trial they were acquired in (the camera only\n   369→    runs during a trial), so a window that extends outside its own trial cannot pick up\n   370→    frames belonging to a neighbouring trial.\n   371→\n   372→    Args:\n   373→        nwb: open NWBFile.\n   374→        trial_start: (n_trials_raw,) absolute trial start times.\n   375→        go: (n_trials,) absolute go-cue times of the retained trials.\n   376→        keep_idx: (n_trials,) indices of the retained trials in the raw trials table.\n   377→\n   378→    Returns:\n   379→        (n_trials, NBINS) float array with the mean visible tongue y per bin (NaN when\n   380→        the tongue was never visible in that bin), and the fraction of visible frames.\n   381→    \"\"\"\n   382→    n_trials = len(keep_idx)\n   383→    out = np.full((n_trials, NBINS), np.nan, dtype=np.float64)\n   384→\n   385→    bts = nwb.acquisition.get('BehavioralTimeSeries', None)\n   386→    if bts is None or 'Camera0_side_TongueTracking' not in bts.time_series:\n   387→        return out, 0.0\n   388→\n   389→    ts_obj = bts.time_series['Camera0_side_TongueTracking']\n   390→    frame_t = np.asarray(ts_obj.timestamps[:], dtype=np.float64)\n   391→    data = np.asarray(ts_obj.data[:, 1:3], dtype=np.float64)     # y, likelihood\n   392→    y = data[:, 0]\n   393→    visible = data[:, 1] > TONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESH\n   394→    visible_frac = float(visible.mean()) if len(visible) else 0.0\n   395→\n   396→    # frame -> trial (video only runs within a trial; frames start at trial_start)\n   397→    frame_trial = np.searchsorted(trial_start, frame_t, side='right') - 1\n   398→\n   399→    # only visible frames matter for the value; keep them and their trial/bin index\n   400→    vis = np.flatnonzero(visible & (frame_trial >= 0))\n   401→    if len(vis) == 0:\n   402→        return out, visible_frac\n   403→    ft = frame_trial[vis]\n   404→\n   405→    # map raw trial index -> position in keep_idx (-1 when the trial was dropped)\n   406→    n_raw = len(trial_start)\n   407→    pos_of_trial = np.full(n_raw, -1, dtype=np.int64)\n   408→    pos_of_trial[keep_idx] = np.arange(n_trials)\n   409→    pos = pos_of_trial[ft]\n   410→    sel = pos >= 0\n   411→    vis, pos = vis[sel], pos[sel]\n   412→    if len(vis) == 0:\n   413→        return out, visible_frac\n   414→\n   415→    rel = frame_t[vis] - go[pos]\n   416→    inwin = (rel >= OFF_START) & (rel < OFF_END)\n   417→    vis, pos, rel = vis[inwin], pos[inwin], rel[inwin]\n   418→    if len(vis) == 0:\n   419→        return out, visible_frac\n   420→\n   421→    bin_idx = np.floor((rel - OFF_START) / BIN_SIZE).astype(np.int64)\n   422→    np.clip(bin_idx, 0, NBINS - 1, out=bin_idx)\n   423→    flat = pos * NBINS + bin_idx\n   424→    n_cells = n_trials * NBINS\n   425→    sums = np.bincount(flat, weights=y[vis], minlength=n_cells)\n   426→    cnts = np.bincount(flat, minlength=n_cells)\n   427→    with np.errstate(invalid='ignore', divide='ignore'):\n   428→        mean = np.where(cnts > 0, sums / np.maximum(cnts, 1), np.nan)\n   429→    out = mean.reshape(n_trials, NBINS)\n   430→    return out, visible_frac\n   431→\n   432→\n   433→def discretise_tongue(tongue_y):\n   434→    \"\"\"Discretise tongue y into 0/1/2 by session percentiles, 3 where not visible.\"\"\"\n   435→    cls = np.full(tongue_y.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)\n   436→    vis = ~np.isnan(tongue_y)\n   437→    if vis.sum() == 0:\n   438→        return cls, (np.nan, np.nan)\n   439→    vals = tongue_y[vis]\n   440→    p_lo = np.percentile(vals, TONGUE_LOW_PCT)\n   441→    p_hi = np.percentile(vals, TONGUE_HIGH_PCT)\n   442→    v = tongue_y[vis]\n   443→    c = np.ones(v.shape, dtype=np.int64)\n   444→    c[v < p_lo] = 0\n   445→    c[v > p_hi] = 2\n   446→    cls[vis] = c\n   447→    return cls, (float(p_lo), float(p_hi))\n   448→\n   449→\n   450→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   451→# Plotting (--show-processing)\n   452→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   453→def plot_processing(res, path):\n   454→    \"\"\"Plot every processing step for one session so it can be checked visually.\"\"\"\n   455→    import matplotlib\n   456→    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   457→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   458→\n   459→    dbg = res['debug']\n   460→    fr = dbg['fr']\n   461→    n_trials = res['n_trials']\n   462→    t = BIN_CENTERS\n   463→\n   464→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(20, 20))\n   465→\n   466→    # (1) trial structure relative to the go cue\n   467→    a = ax[0, 0]\n   468→    rel_start = dbg['trial_start'] - dbg['go']\n   469→    rel_stop = dbg['trial_stop'] - dbg['go']\n   470→    rel_tone = dbg['tone_time'] - dbg['go']\n   471→    a.plot(rel_start, np.arange(n_trials), '.', ms=2, label='trial start')\n   472→    a.plot(rel_stop, np.arange(n_trials), '.', ms=2, label='trial stop (end of recording)')\n   473→    a.plot(rel_tone, np.arange(n_trials), '.', ms=2, label='tone (sample) onset')\n   474→    a.axvline(0, color='k', label='go cue')\n   475→    a.axvline(OFF_START, color='r', ls='--'), a.axvline(OFF_END, color='r', ls='--')\n   476→    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'), a.set_ylabel('trial'), a.legend(fontsize=7)\n   477→    a.set_title('Step 1: temporal alignment (red = extraction window)')\n   478→\n   479→    # (2) population PSTH, sorted by choice\n   480→    a = ax[0, 1]\n   481→    order = np.argsort(dbg['choice'], kind='stable')\n   482→    pop = fr.mean(axis=0)[order]\n   483→    im = a.imshow(pop, aspect='auto', origin='lower',\n   484→                  extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, n_trials], cmap='magma')\n   485→    a.axvline(0, color='w')\n   486→    plt.colorbar(im, ax=a, label='pop. mean rate (Hz)')\n   487→    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'), a.set_ylabel('trial (sorted by choice)')\n   488→    a.set_title('Step 2: binned firing rates (50 ms bins)')\n   489→\n   490→    # (3) mean rate of the 40 most active neurons\n   491→    a = ax[1, 0]\n   492→    mean_fr = fr.mean(axis=(1, 2))\n   493→    top = np.argsort(-mean_fr)[:40]\n   494→    for i in top:\n   495→        a.plot(t, fr[i].mean(axis=0), lw=0.7)\n   496→    a.axvline(0, color='k')\n   497→    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'), a.set_ylabel('mean rate (Hz)')\n   498→    a.set_title('Step 2b: trial-averaged rates, 40 most active neurons')\n   499→\n   500→    # (4) input 0: time from tone onset\n   501→    a = ax[1, 1]\n   502→    for j in range(0, n_trials, max(1, n_trials // 40)):\n   503→        a.plot(t, dbg['time_from_tone'][j], lw=0.7)\n   504→    a.axvline(0, color='k'), a.axhline(0, color='r', ls='--')\n   505→    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'), a.set_ylabel('time from tone onset (s)')\n   506→    a.set_title('Step 3: input 0 (red dashed = tone onset)')\n   507→\n   508→    # (5) input 1: photostim\n   509→    a = ax[2, 0]\n   510→    im = a.imshow(dbg['photostim_on'], aspect='auto', origin='lower',\n   511→                  extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, n_trials], cmap='gray_r')\n   512→    a.axvline(0, color='r')\n   513→    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'), a.set_ylabel('trial')\n   514→    a.set_title('Step 3b: input 1, photostim on (%d stim trials)'\n   515→                % int((dbg['photostim_on'].max(axis=1) > 0).sum()))\n   516→\n   517→    # (6) tongue y + discretisation\n   518→    a = ax[2, 1]\n   519→    ty = dbg['tongue_y']\n   520→    vis = ~np.isnan(ty)\n   521→    a.hist(ty[vis], bins=80, color='0.6')\n   522→    for p, c in zip(res['tongue_percentiles'], ['b', 'g']):\n   523→        a.axvline(p, color=c, lw=2)\n   524→    a.set_xlabel('tongue y (px)'), a.set_ylabel('# bins')\n   525→    a.set_title('Step 4: tongue-y distribution (visible bins) + 40/60 pct')\n   526→\n   527→    # (7) tongue class raster\n   528→    a = ax[3, 0]\n   529→    im = a.imshow(dbg['tongue_class'], aspect='auto', origin='lower',\n   530→                  extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, n_trials], cmap='viridis',\n   531→                  vmin=-0.5, vmax=3.5, interpolation='nearest')\n   532→    a.axvline(0, color='r')\n   533→    plt.colorbar(im, ax=a, ticks=[0, 1, 2, 3], label='tongue class')\n   534→    a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'), a.set_ylabel('trial')\n   535→    a.set_title('Step 4b: output 3, tongue class (3 = not visible)')\n   536→\n   537→    # (8) per-trial outputs\n   538→    a = ax[3, 1]\n   539→    a.plot(dbg['choice'], np.arange(n_trials), '.', ms=3, label='choice (0=L,1=R,2=none)')\n   540→    a.plot(np.array([OUTCOME_CODE[o] for o in dbg['outcome']]) + 0.15, np.arange(n_trials),\n   541→           '.', ms=3, label='outcome (0=ign,1=miss,2=hit)')\n   542→    a.plot((dbg['early'] == 'early').astype(int) + 0.3, np.arange(n_trials), '.', ms=3,\n   543→           label='early lick')\n   544→    a.set_xlabel('value'), a.set_ylabel('trial'), a.legend(fontsize=7)\n   545→    a.set_title('Step 4c: outputs 0-2 (per trial)')\n   546→\n   547→    fig.suptitle('%s  (%d neurons, %d trials)' % (res['session_id'], res['n_neurons'], n_trials))\n   548→    fig.tight_layout()\n   549→    fig.savefig(path, dpi=110)\n   550→    plt.close(fig)\n   551→    print(f'  wrote {path}', flush=True)\n   552→\n   553→\n   554→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   555→# Driver\n   556→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   557→def _worker(args):\n   558→    path, debug = args\n   559→    try:\n   560→        return process_session(path, collect_debug=debug)\n   561→    except Exception as exc:          # keep one bad file from killing the whole run\n   562→        import traceback\n   563→        traceback.print_exc()\n   564→        print(f'  ERROR processing {path}: {exc}', flush=True)\n   565→        return None\n   566→\n   567→\n   568→def main():\n   569→    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__,\n   570→                                 formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)\n   571→    ap.add_argument('outfile')\n   572→    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True)\n   573→    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true')\n   574→    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true')\n   575→    ap.add_argument('--nproc', type=int, default=16)\n   576→    args = ap.parse_args()\n   577→\n   578→    files = list_session_files()\n   579→    if args.sample:\n   580→        files = files[:2]\n   581→    print(f'Found {len(files)} session files', flush=True)\n   582→\n   583→    t_start = time.time()\n   584→    debug_for = set(files[:2]) if args.show_processing else set()\n   585→\n   586→    jobs = [(f, f in debug_for) for f in files]\n   587→    results = []\n   588→    if args.nproc > 1 and len(files) > 1:\n   589→        import multiprocessing as mp\n   590→        ctx = mp.get_context('spawn')\n   591→        with ctx.Pool(min(args.nproc, len(files))) as pool:\n   592→            for i, res in enumerate(pool.imap(_worker, jobs)):\n   593→                if res is None:\n   594→                    print(f'[{i + 1}/{len(files)}] dropped {os.path.basename(jobs[i][0])}',\n   595→                          flush=True)\n   596→                else:\n   597→                    print('[%d/%d] %s: %d neurons, %d/%d trials (%.1fs: %s)'\n   598→                          % (i + 1, len(files), res['session_id'], res['n_neurons'],\n   599→                             res['n_trials'], res['n_trials_raw'], res['total_time'],\n   600→                             ', '.join(f'{k}={v:.1f}' for k, v in res['timing'].items())),\n   601→                          flush=True)\n   602→                    results.append(res)\n   603→    else:\n   604→        for i, job in enumerate(jobs):\n   605→            res = _worker(job)\n   606→            if res is None:\n   607→                print(f'[{i + 1}/{len(files)}] dropped {os.path.basename(job[0])}', flush=True)\n   608→                continue\n   609→            print('[%d/%d] %s: %d neurons, %d/%d trials (%.1fs: %s)'\n   610→                  % (i + 1, len(files), res['session_id'], res['n_neurons'], res['n_trials'],\n   611→                     res['n_trials_raw'], res['total_time'],\n   612→                     ', '.join(f'{k}={v:.1f}' for k, v in res['timing'].items())), flush=True)\n   613→            results.append(res)\n   614→\n   615→    t_convert = time.time() - t_start\n   616→ python3\n\"\"\"\nConvert the MAP brain-wide Neuropixels dataset (DANDI:000363, NWB) into the decoder\nformat described in the task specification.\n\nUsage\n-----\n    python -u /app/convert_data.py <outpicklefile> [--full | --sample] [--show-processing]\n\nOptions\n-------\n    --full             process all sessions (default)\n    --sample           process only 2 sessions (for testing)\n    --show-processing  plot every processing step for up to 2 sessions,\n                       saved as processing_<session_id>.png\n    --nproc N          number of worker processes (default: 16)\n\nDesign decisions (see /app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md for the full rationale)\n---------------------------------------------------------------------\n* Alignment event: the auditory **go cue** (`BehavioralEvents['go_start_times']`).\n* Window: [-2.5, +1.5] s around the go cue, 80 non-overlapping 50 ms bins.\n* Neural data: firing **rate** in Hz (spike count / 0.05 s), as in the reference\n  `sliding_histogram(..., rate=True)`.\n* Units: only `classification == 'good'` (the region-specific QC classifiers of the\n  data paper / QC white paper).\n* Trials: `auto_water` and `free_water` trials are dropped (reference\n  `get_regular_trial_mask`), as are trials not annotated good in `is_good_trials`.\n  Early-lick / `ignore` / photostim trials are KEPT because they are required by the\n  decoder input & output specification.\n* Sessions: dropped if they contain no good units or fewer than 2 usable trials.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport pickle\nimport annotation_to_region  # noqa: E402\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Constants\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nDATA_DIR = '/app/data'\n\nOFF_START = -2.5          # s, signed time from the go cue to the start of the trial window\nOFF_END = 1.5             # s, signed time from the go cue to the end of the trial window\nBIN_SIZE = 0.05           # s\nNBINS = int(round((OFF_END - OFF_START) / BIN_SIZE))          # 80\nBIN_EDGES = OFF_START + BIN_SIZE * np.arange(NBINS + 1)       # (81,)\nBIN_CENTERS = BIN_EDGES[:-1] + BIN_SIZE / 2                   # (80,)\n\nTONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESH = 0.9   # DeepLabCut likelihood above which the tongue is \"visible\"\nTONGUE_LOW_PCT = 40              # percentile boundaries for the tongue-y discretisation\nTONGUE_HIGH_PCT = 60\n\nML_MIDLINE_UM = 5700             # reference rule: CCF x >= 5700 um => left hemisphere\nOVERLAP_TOL = 1e-3               # s, minimum overlap for a bin to count as photostim-on\n\nINPUT_NAMES = ['time_from_tone_onset', 'photostim_on']\nOUTPUT_NAMES = ['choice', 'outcome', 'early_lick', 'tongue_y_position']\nOUTPUT_VALUES = [\n    ['left', 'right', 'no lick'],\n    ['ignore', 'miss', 'hit'],\n    ['no', 'yes'],\n    ['low (<40th pct)', 'mid (40-60th pct)', 'high (>60th pct)', 'not visible'],\n]\n\nCHOICE_LEFT, CHOICE_RIGHT, CHOICE_NOLICK = 0, 1, 2\nOUTCOME_CODE = {'ignore': 0, 'miss': 1, 'hit': 2}\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Helpers\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef list_session_files(data_dir=DATA_DIR):\n    \"\"\"Return the sorted list of NWB session files.\"\"\"\n    files = []\n    for sub in sorted(os.listdir(data_dir)):\n        d = os.path.join(data_dir, sub)\n        if not os.path.isdir(d):\n            continue\n        for f in sorted(os.listdir(d)):\n            if f.endswith('.nwb'):\n                files.append(os.path.join(d, f))\n    return files\n\n\ndef bin_spikes_rate(spike_times, go_times):\n    \"\"\"Spike counts of one unit in the fixed window around every go cue, as a rate.\n\n    Args:\n        spike_times: (n_spikes,) sorted absolute spike times of one unit, in seconds.\n        go_times: (n_trials,) absolute go-cue times, in seconds.\n\n    Returns:\n        (n_trials, NBINS) float32 array of firing rates in Hz.\n\n    Notes:\n        `np.searchsorted` is used on the (sorted) spike train, so the cost is\n        O(n_trials * NBINS * log n_spikes) and no per-trial Python loop is needed.\n        Bin k covers [go + BIN_EDGES[k], go + BIN_EDGES[k+1]).\n    \"\"\"\n    edges = go_times[:, None] + BIN_EDGES[None, :]           # (n_trials, NBINS+1)\n    idx = np.searchsorted(spike_times, edges.ravel(), side='left')\n    idx = idx.reshape(edges.shape)\n    counts = np.diff(idx, axis=1)\n    return (counts / BIN_SIZE).astype(np.float32)\n\n\ndef interval_overlap_bins(starts, stops, go_time):\n    \"\"\"Binary (NBINS,) indicator: 1 where a [start, stop] interval overlaps the bin.\n\n    Args:\n        starts, stops: absolute start/stop times of the intervals (may be empty).\n        go_time: absolute time of this trial's go cue.\n    \"\"\"\n    on = np.zeros(NBINS, dtype=np.float32)\n    if len(starts) == 0:\n        return on\n    for a, b in zip(starts - go_time, stops - go_time):\n        if b <= BIN_EDGES[0] or a >= BIN_EDGES[-1]:\n            continue\n        # Overlap duration between [a, b] and every bin.  A plain \"does it touch the bin\"\n        # test would mark a bin that the interval overlaps by a fraction of a millisecond:\n        # photoinhibition ends at the go cue to within +-0.5 ms, which would otherwise\n        # switch the first post-go bin on for about half of the stimulated trials.\n        ov = np.minimum(b, BIN_EDGES[1:]) - np.maximum(a, BIN_EDGES[:-1])\n        on[ov > OVERLAP_TOL] = 1.0\n    return on\n\n\ndef _events(nwb, name):\n    \"\"\"Timestamps of a `BehavioralEvents` TimeSeries, as a numpy array.\"\"\"\n    ts = nwb.acquisition['BehavioralEvents'].time_series[name]\n    return np.asarray(ts.timestamps[:], dtype=np.float64)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Per-session conversion\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef process_session(path, collect_debug=False):\n    \"\"\"Convert one NWB session.\n\n    Args:\n        path: path to the NWB file.\n        collect_debug: if True, also return intermediate quantities for plotting.\n\n    Returns:\n        dict with the converted session, or None if the session has to be dropped.\n    \"\"\"\n    from pynwb import NWBHDF5IO\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    timing = {}\n    with warnings.catch_warnings():\n        warnings.simplefilter('ignore')\n        io = NWBHDF5IO(path, 'r', load_namespaces=True)\n        nwb = io.read()\n\n        session_id = nwb.identifier\n        subject = str(nwb.subject.subject_id)\n\n        # ---------------- trials table -------------------------------------------\n        trials = nwb.trials\n        n_trials_raw = len(trials)\n        instruction = np.asarray(trials['trial_instruction'][:])\n        outcome = np.asarray(trials['outcome'][:])\n        early_lick = np.asarray(trials['early_lick'][:])\n        auto_water = np.asarray(trials['auto_water'][:]).astype(bool)\n        free_water = np.asarray(trials['free_water'][:]).astype(bool)\n        trial_start = np.asarray(trials['start_time'][:], dtype=np.float64)\n        trial_stop = np.asarray(trials['stop_time'][:], dtype=np.float64)\n\n        go_times = _events(nwb, 'go_start_times')\n        if len(go_times) != n_trials_raw:\n            io.close()\n            raise RuntimeError(f'{session_id}: {len(go_times)} go cues for {n_trials_raw} trials')\n\n        # ---------------- units ---------------------------------------------------\n        units = nwb.units\n        classification = np.asarray(units['classification'][:])\n        good = np.flatnonzero(classification == 'good')\n        if len(good) == 0:\n            io.close()\n            return None\n\n        anno = np.asarray(units['anno_name'][:])[good]\n        egroups = np.asarray(units['electrode_group'].data[:])[good]\n        probe_target = np.array([json.loads(g.location)['brain_regions'] for g in egroups])\n\n        # unit -> electrode -> CCF x, for the hemisphere rule of the reference code\n        er = units['electrodes']\n        cum = np.asarray(er.data[:])\n        elec_idx = np.asarray(er.target.data[:])[cum - 1][good]\n        elec_x = np.asarray(nwb.electrodes['x'][:], dtype=np.float64)[elec_idx]\n        hemisphere = np.where(elec_x >= ML_MIDLINE_UM, 'left', 'right')\n        # fall back on the insertion's targeted hemisphere where CCF x is missing\n        missing = np.isnan(elec_x)\n        if missing.any():\n            hemisphere[missing] = np.array([t.split(' ')[0] for t in probe_target])[missing]\n\n        region_name = np.array([annotation_to_region(a, t) for a, t in zip(anno, probe_target)])\n        unknown = region_name == None  # noqa: E711\n        if unknown.any():\n            print(f'  WARNING {session_id}: {unknown.sum()} units with unmapped annotation '\n                  f'{set(anno[unknown])}', flush=True)\n            region_name[unknown] = 'OtherCortex'\n        region_idx = np.array([REGIONS.index(r) for r in region_name], dtype=np.int64)\n\n        # ---------------- trial curation -----------------------------------------\n        # In 9 sessions the ephys recording covers only a subset of the behavioural\n        # trials, so the observed trials have to be located in the trials table first.\n        # units.obs_intervals is identical for every unit of a session and its rows\n        # match the [start_time, stop_time] of the observed trials exactly (verified\n        # for all 174 files).\n        obs = np.asarray(units['obs_intervals'][int(good[0])], dtype=np.float64)\n        obs_trial = np.searchsorted(trial_start, obs[:, 0] - 1e-6)\n        if not np.allclose(trial_start[obs_trial], obs[:, 0]):\n            raise RuntimeError(f'{session_id}: obs_intervals do not match the trials table')\n\n        # (a) reference `get_regular_trial_mask`: no auto-water, no free-water trials\n        keep = (~auto_water[obs_trial]) & (~free_water[obs_trial])\n        # (b) recording-stability annotation: keep only trials annotated 'good' for\n        #     every retained unit (i.e. for every probe insertion contributing units)\n        is_good_trials = np.asarray(units['is_good_trials'][:])[good]   # (n_good, n_obs)\n        keep &= is_good_trials.all(axis=0)\n        keep_idx = obs_trial[keep]\n        n_trials = len(keep_idx)\n        n_trials_obs = len(obs_trial)\n        if n_trials < 2:\n            io.close()\n            return None\n\n        go = go_times[keep_idx]\n        timing['setup'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        # ---------------- neural ---------------------------------------------------\n        t1 = time.time()\n        sv = units['spike_times']\n        ends = np.asarray(sv.data[:])\n        starts = np.concatenate([[0], ends[:-1]])\n        flat_spikes = np.asarray(sv.target.data[:], dtype=np.float64)\n\n        n_neurons = len(good)\n        fr = np.empty((n_neurons, n_trials, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n        for i, u in enumerate(good):\n            st = flat_spikes[starts[u]:ends[u]]\n            fr[i] = bin_spikes_rate(st, go)\n\n        # A trial in which not one of the (hundreds of) good units fired a single spike\n        # means the amplifiers were not running: the recording can stop part-way through\n        # the last annotated trial. Such trials carry no neural information at all.\n        nonempty = fr.sum(axis=(0, 2)) > 0\n        if not nonempty.all():\n            n_empty = int((~nonempty).sum())\n            print(f'  {session_id}: dropping {n_empty} trial(s) with no spikes at all',\n                  flush=True)\n            fr = fr[:, nonempty, :]\n            keep_idx = keep_idx[nonempty]\n            go = go[nonempty]\n            n_trials = len(keep_idx)\n            if n_trials < 2:\n                io.close()\n                return None\n        timing['neural'] = time.time() - t1\n\n        # ---------------- inputs ---------------------------------------------------\n        t1 = time.time()\n        # tone onset := last sample-epoch start before the go cue\n        sample_start = _events(nwb, 'sample_start_times')\n        tone_pos = np.searchsorted(sample_start, go, side='right') - 1\n        if np.any(tone_pos < 0):\n            raise RuntimeError(f'{session_id}: trial without a preceding sample epoch')\n        tone_time = sample_start[tone_pos]\n        # time from tone onset at each bin centre (seconds, continuous)\n        time_from_tone = BIN_CENTERS[None, :] + (go - tone_time)[:, None]   # (n_trials, NBINS)\n\n        photostim_on = np.zeros((n_trials, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n        try:\n            ps_start = _events(nwb, 'photostim_start_times')\n            ps_stop = _events(nwb, 'photostim_stop_times')\n        except KeyError:\n            ps_start = ps_stop = np.zeros(0)\n        if len(ps_start) and len(ps_start) == len(ps_stop):\n            for j, g in enumerate(go):\n                m = (ps_stop > g + OFF_START) & (ps_start < g + OFF_END)\n                if m.any():\n                    photostim_on[j] = interval_overlap_bins(ps_start[m], ps_stop[m], g)\n        elif len(ps_start) != len(ps_stop):\n            print(f'  WARNING {session_id}: photostim start/stop counts differ '\n                  f'({len(ps_start)}/{len(ps_stop)}); photostim input left at 0', flush=True)\n        timing['input'] = time.time() - t1\n\n        # ---------------- outputs --------------------------------------------------\n        t1 = time.time()\n        instr_k = instruction[keep_idx]\n        out_k = outcome[keep_idx]\n        early_k = early_lick[keep_idx]\n\n        # choice: hit -> instructed side, miss -> opposite side, ignore -> no lick\n        choice = np.full(n_trials, CHOICE_NOLICK, dtype=np.int64)\n        hit = out_k == 'hit'\n        miss = out_k == 'miss'\n        choice[hit & (instr_k == 'left')] = CHOICE_LEFT\n        choice[hit & (instr_k == 'right')] = CHOICE_RIGHT\n        choice[miss & (instr_k == 'left')] = CHOICE_RIGHT\n        choice[miss & (instr_k == 'right')] = CHOICE_LEFT\n\n        outcome_code = np.array([OUTCOME_CODE[o] for o in out_k], dtype=np.int64)\n        early_code = (early_k == 'early').astype(np.int64)\n\n        tongue_y_binned, tongue_visible_frac = bin_tongue(nwb, trial_start, go, keep_idx)\n        tongue_class, pct = discretise_tongue(tongue_y_binned)\n        timing['output'] = time.time() - t1\n\n        io.close()\n\n    # ---------------- assemble ----------------------------------------------------\n    neural = [fr[:, j, :] for j in range(n_trials)]\n    inputs = [np.stack([time_from_tone[j], photostim_on[j]]).astype(np.float32)\n              for j in range(n_trials)]\n    outputs = [np.stack([np.full(NBINS, choice[j]),\n                         np.full(NBINS, outcome_code[j]),\n                         np.full(NBINS, early_code[j]),\n                         tongue_class[j]]).astype(np.int64)\n               for j in range(n_trials)]\n\n    result = dict(\n        session_id=session_id,\n        subject=subject,\n        file=os.path.basename(path),\n        neural=neural,\n        input=inputs,\n        output=outputs,\n        brain_region_idx=region_idx,\n        hemisphere=np.where(hemisphere == 'left', 0, 1).astype(np.int8),\n        n_trials_raw=n_trials_raw,\n        n_trials_observed=n_trials_obs,\n        n_trials=n_trials,\n        n_neurons=n_neurons,\n        trial_index=keep_idx,                     # index into the raw trials table\n        tongue_percentiles=pct,\n        tongue_visible_frac=float(tongue_visible_frac),\n        timing=timing,\n        total_time=time.time() - t0,\n    )\n    if collect_debug:\n        result['debug'] = dict(\n            go=go, tongue_class=tongue_class,\n            fr=fr, instruction=instr_k, outcome=out_k, early=early_k,\n            choice=choice, anno=anno, region_name=region_name, hemisphere=hemisphere,\n        )\n    return result\n\n\ndef bin_tongue(nwb, trial_start, go, keep_idx):\n    \"\"\"Mean tongue y-position per 50 ms bin, NaN where the tongue is not visible.\n\n    Video frames are first assigned to the trial they were acquired in (the camera only\n    runs during a trial), so a window that extends outside its own trial cannot pick up\n    frames belonging to a neighbouring trial.\n\n    Args:\n        nwb: open NWBFile.\n        trial_start: (n_trials_raw,) absolute trial start times.\n        go: (n_trials,) absolute go-cue times of the retained trials.\n        keep_idx: (n_trials,) indices of the retained trials in the raw trials table.\n\n    Returns:\n        (n_trials, NBINS) float array with the mean visible tongue y per bin (NaN when\n        the tongue was never visible in that bin), and the fraction of visible frames.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(keep_idx)\n    out = np.full((n_trials, NBINS), np.nan, dtype=np.float64)\n\n    bts = nwb.acquisition.get('BehavioralTimeSeries', None)\n    if bts is None or 'Camera0_side_TongueTracking' not in bts.time_series:\n        return out, 0.0\n\n    ts_obj = bts.time_series['Camera0_side_TongueTracking']\n    frame_t = np.asarray(ts_obj.timestamps[:], dtype=np.float64)\n    data = np.asarray(ts_obj.data[:, 1:3], dtype=np.float64)     # y, likelihood\n    y = data[:, 0]\n    visible = data[:, 1] > TONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESH\n    visible_frac = float(visible.mean()) if len(visible) else 0.0\n\n    # frame -> trial (video only runs within a trial; frames start at trial_start)\n    frame_trial = np.searchsorted(trial_start, frame_t, side='right') - 1\n\n    # only visible frames matter for the value; keep them and their trial/bin index\n    vis = np.flatnonzero(visible & (frame_trial >= 0))\n    if len(vis) == 0:\n        return out, visible_frac\n    ft = frame_trial[vis]\n\n    # map raw trial index -> position in keep_idx (-1 when the trial was dropped)\n    n_raw = len(trial_start)\n    pos_of_trial = np.full(n_raw, -1, dtype=np.int64)\n    pos_of_trial[keep_idx] = np.arange(n_trials)\n    pos = pos_of_trial[ft]\n    sel = pos >= 0\n    vis, pos = vis[sel], pos[sel]\n    if len(vis) == 0:\n        return out, visible_frac\n\n    rel = frame_t[vis] - go[pos]\n    inwin = (rel >= OFF_START) & (rel < OFF_END)\n    vis, pos, rel = vis[inwin], pos[inwin], rel[inwin]\n    if len(vis) == 0:\n        return out, visible_frac\n\n    bin_idx = np.floor((rel - OFF_START) / BIN_SIZE).astype(np.int64)\n    np.clip(bin_idx, 0, NBINS - 1, out=bin_idx)\n    flat = pos * NBINS + bin_idx\n    n_cells = n_trials * NBINS\n    sums = np.bincount(flat, weights=y[vis], minlength=n_cells)\n    cnts = np.bincount(flat, minlength=n_cells)\n    with np.errstate(invalid='ignore', divide='ignore'):\n        mean = np.where(cnts > 0, sums / np.maximum(cnts, 1), np.nan)\n    out = mean.reshape(n_trials, NBINS)\n    return out, visible_frac\n\n\ndef discretise_tongue(tongue_y):\n    \"\"\"Discretise tongue y into 0/1/2 by session percentiles, 3 where not visible.\"\"\"\n    cls = np.full(tongue_y.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)\n    vis = ~np.isnan(tongue_y)\n    if vis.sum() == 0:\n        return cls, (np.nan, np.nan)\n    vals = tongue_y[vis]\n    p_lo = np.percentile(vals, TONGUE_LOW_PCT)\n    p_hi = np.percentile(vals, TONGUE_HIGH_PCT)\n    v = tongue_y[vis]\n    c = np.ones(v.shape, dtype=np.int64)\n    c[v < p_lo] = 0\n    c[v > p_hi] = 2\n    cls[vis] = c\n    return cls, (float(p_lo), float(p_hi))\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Plotting (--show-processing)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef plot_processing(res, path):\n    \"\"\"Plot every processing step for one session so it can be checked The\n    36→DataJoint export and the NWB release contain the same underlying quantities, so the reference\n    37→code tells us **which** variables are used and **how** they are processed.\n    38→\n    39→### Key Functions Identified\n    40→| Function | File | Stage | Purpose |\n    41→|----------|------|-------|---------|\n    42→| `preprocessing_utils.loadmat` | `VideoAnalysisUtils/preprocessing_utils.py` | LOADING | Load one probe's `.mat` export (replaced here by `pynwb`) |\n    43→| `preprocessing.process_one_sess` | `VideoAnalysisUtils/preprocessing_DJ_2022Aug.py` | LOADING/CURATION | Concatenate all probes of a session; read behaviour/task variables; apply QC good-unit index; split by brain area and hemisphere |\n    44→| `preprocessing.helper_get_neuron_id_area` | same | CURATION | Intersect QC good-unit list with hemisphere (`ccf_x >= 5700` ⇒ left) and require a non-empty CCF annotation |\n    45→| `preprocessing.process_one_area` | same | PROCESSING | Truncate per-trial spike times to `[begin_time, end_time]` **relative to the go cue** and bin them |\n    46→| `preprocessing.sliding_histogram` | same | PROCESSING | Spike counts in sliding bins of width `bw` strided by `stride`; `rate=True` ⇒ divide by `bw` to get **firing rate in Hz** |\n    47→| `Sherlock/preprocess_all_ephys.py` | — | PROCESSING | Top-level parameters actually used for the paper: `bw = 0.04`, `stride = 0.0034`, `begin_time = -3.0`, `end_time = 3.0`, `qc_mode = 'classifier'` |\n    48→| `functions_for_r2.get_regular_trial_mask` / `population_decoding_utils.get_regular_trial_mask` | `VideoAnalysisUtils/…` | CURATION | Trial mask: `early_lick == 0`, `auto_water == 0`, `free_water == 0`, `correctness != -1` (no-response), `stimulation[:,0] == 0` (no photostim) |\n    49→| `functions_for_r2.create_4fold_trial_type_mask` | `VideoAnalysisUtils/functions_for_r2.py` | PROCESSING | Defines the (trial_type, correctness) contingencies used for stratification; documents `trial_type` 1 = left, 0 = right and `correctness` 1/0/−1 = correct/error/no-response |\n    50→| `Sherlock/align_markers.py` | — | LOADING/PROCESSING | Reads DeepLabCut markers `['nose_x','nose_y','tongue_x','tongue_y','jaw_x','jaw_y','whisker_x','whisker_y']` from `tracking.camera_0_side`; aligns them to the **go cue** over `t ∈ [−3, 1.5)` at `dt = 0.0034` s (300 Hz video) |\n    51→| `functions_for_r2.temporal_alignment_embed_and_ephys` | `VideoAnalysisUtils/functions_for_r2.py` | PROCESSING | Aligns the video time base and the ephys time base on a common grid |\n    52→| `preprocessing_utils.get_period` | `VideoAnalysisUtils/preprocessing_utils.py` | — | Epoch definitions relative to the go cue: sample `[−1.9, −1.2]`, delay `[−1.2, 0]`, post-go `[0, 1.0]` |\n    53→\n    54→### Notes\n    55→- **Electrophysiology, not imaging** ⇒ no ΔF/F. Cells **do** have to be quality-filtered.\n    56→- Quality control is `qc_mode='classifier'`: the region-specific logistic-regression classifiers\n    57→  described in `methods.txt` and the QC white paper. Their output is a per-unit `'good'` /\n    58→  `'unlabelled'` label. In NWB this is the `units.classification` column — no need to re-derive\n    59→  it from the 15 raw quality metrics (which are also present in the file).\n    60→- The reference also requires a unit to have a CCF annotation (`ccf_label[idx] != []`). In NWB,\n    61→  `units.anno_name` is non-empty **exactly** for units with `classification == 'good'`\n    62→  (verified: 0 good units with empty annotation, 0 unlabelled units with an annotation, in the\n    63→  example session), so the two criteria coincide.\n    64→- Hemisphere rule (reference): CCF `x >= 5700 µm` ⇒ left hemisphere, else right.\n    65→- Brain areas used by the reference (14): `ALM, Medulla, Midbrain, Striatum, Thalamus, Pons,\n    66→  Cerebellum, Hypothalamus, Hippocampus, Orbital, OtherCortex, Olfactory, CorticalSubplate,\n    67→  Pallidum`, each split into `left_`/`right_`.\n    68→- Spike times in the reference `.mat` export are **already relative to the go cue**; in NWB they\n    69→  are absolute session times, so we must subtract `go_start_times` ourselves.\n    70→- The reference bins with *sliding* windows (40 ms width, 3.4 ms stride) because it needs the\n    71→  neural signal on the 300 Hz video grid. Our task specifies **50 ms bins**, so we use\n    72→  non-overlapping 50 ms bins (width = stride = 50 ms) and keep `rate=True` (Hz), which is the\n    73→  only deviation from `sliding_histogram`.\n    74→\n    75→---\n    76→\n    77→## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n    78→**Status**: COMPLETE\n    79→\n    80→### Data Structure\n    81→```\n    82→/app/data/\n    83→  dandiset.yaml\n    84→  sub-<subject_id>/                      # 29 dirs, 28 of them subjects (+ dandiset.yaml)\n    85→    sub-<id>_ses-<YYYYmmddTHHMMSS>_behavior+ecephys+ogen.nwb\n    86→```\n    87→174 NWB files, one per behavioural session. Each file:\n    88→\n    89→- `nwb.subject.subject_id` (e.g. `440956`), `nwb.identifier` (e.g. `SC015_20190207_120657_s1`).\n    90→- `nwb.trials` (`TimeIntervals`), columns:\n    91→  `start_time, stop_time, trial, photostim_onset, photostim_power, photostim_duration,\n    92→   trial_uid, task, task_protocol, trial_instruction, early_lick, outcome, auto_water, free_water`.\n    93→  - `task` is `'audio delay'` and `task_protocol` is `1` for **all** 174 sessions.\n    94→  - `trial_instruction ∈ {'left','right'}`; `early_lick ∈ {'early','no early'}`;\n    95→    `outcome ∈ {'hit','miss','ignore'}`; `auto_water`, `free_water ∈ {0,1}`.\n    96→  - `photostim_*` are strings, `'N/A'` when the trial had no photostimulation.\n    97→- `nwb.units` (1300–2500 rows/session), columns include\n    98→  `unit, unit_quality, unit_amp, unit_snr, isi_violation, avg_firing_rate, drift_metric,\n    99→   presence_ratio, amplitude_cutoff, isolation_distance, l_ratio, d_prime, nn_hit_rate,\n   100→   nn_miss_rate, silhouette_score, max_drift, cumulative_drift, duration, halfwidth, …,\n   101→   classification, anno_name, is_good_trials, spike_times, obs_intervals, electrodes,\n   102→   electrode_group, waveform_mean, waveform_sd`.\n   103→  - `classification` ∈ `{'good','unlabelled'}` — the QC-classifier label.\n   104→  - `anno_name` — CCF (Allen) annotation of the unit (293 distinct names in the dataset).\n   105→  - `is_good_trials` — per-unit boolean vector of length `n_trials`, \"manually annotated 'good'\n   106→    trials for a particular probe insertion\" (recording-stability annotation).\n   107→  - `spike_times` — **absolute session time**; `obs_intervals` — `(n_trials, 2)`, exactly equal to\n   108→    the trials table `start_time`/`stop_time` and identical for every unit in a session.\n   109→- `nwb.acquisition['BehavioralEvents']` — `TimeSeries` whose *timestamps* carry the events:\n   110→  `presample/sample/delay/go/trialend _start_times` and `_stop_times`,\n   111→  `left_lick_times`, `right_lick_times`, `photostim_start_times` (`data` = power in mW),\n   112→  `photostim_stop_times`. `go_start_times` has exactly one timestamp per trial in all 174 files.\n   113→- `nwb.acquisition['BehavioralTimeSeries']` — DeepLabCut markers, absolute timestamps, 300 Hz:\n   114→  `Camera0_side_JawTracking`, `Camera0_side_NoseTracking`, `Camera0_side_TongueTracking`\n   115→  (each `(n_frames, 3)` = `x, y, likelihood`). Present in **all 174 sessions**.\n   116→  20 sessions additionally have `Camera0_side_WhiskerTracking_whisker`, 4 have\n   117→  `Camera0_side_LickPortTracking`, 3 have a second camera `Camera3_side_*`.\n   118→- `nwb.electrodes` — CCF coordinates `x,y,z` per electrode; `nwb.electrode_groups[*].location`\n   119→  is a JSON string with the *targeted* `brain_regions` (e.g. `\"left ALM\"`) and stereotaxic\n   120→  coordinates.\n   121→\n   122→### Dataset Size (from data files)\n   123→| Statistic | Value |\n   124→|-----------|-------|\n   125→| NWB files (sessions) | 174 |\n   126→| Sessions with ≥1 `good` unit | **173** |\n   127→| Subjects | **28** |\n   128→| Sessions / subject | mean 6.2 (range 4–9) |\n   129→| Probe insertions (electrode groups) | 659 total; **655** excluding the session with 0 good units |\n   130→| Units (all) / session | 1300–2500 |\n   131→| `good` units (total) | **69,453** |\n   132→| `good` units / session | mean 399 (range 0–923) |\n   133→| Trials (total, raw) | 94,990 |\n   134→| Trials / session (raw) | mean 545.9 (range 264–800) |\n   135→| Trials / session excluding early-lick | mean 483.8 (range 209–695) |\n   136→\n   137→Probe target regions (insertions): left/right ALM 108/103, Striatum 55/59, Thalamus 51/40,\n   138→Midbrain 68/68, Medulla 31/27, ECT 18/14, BLA 9/8.\n   139→\n   140→---\n   141→\n   142→## Step 3: Reference Text Reading\n   143→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   144→\n   145→### Expected Statistics (from papers/methods)\n   146→| Statistic | Value | Source Quote |\n   147→|-----------|-------|--------------|\n   148→| Subjects | 28 | \"Mice (n = 28, Table S1)\"; method paper: \"data from 28 mice\" |\n   149→| Behavioral sessions | 173 | \"69,943 good units recorded across 173 behavioral sessions\" |\n   150→| Probe insertions | 655 (660 penetrations in Fig 1J) | \"from which 655 probe insertions were made\" |\n   151→| Probes / session | 2 probes × 2, 3 × 53, 4 × 98, 5 × 20 sessions (= 173 sessions, 655 insertions) | Results |\n   152→| Good units (total) | 69,943 | \"the dataset consisted of 69,943 good units\" |\n   153→| Good units by area | ALM 8717, striatum 7664, thalamus 12808, midbrain 7495, medulla 2928 | methods.txt |\n   154→| Fraction of KS2 clusters kept | 25.9 % | \"This corresponds to 25.9 % of clusters reported by Kilosort2\" |\n   155→| Trials / session | mean 476, range 130–785 | \"Mice performed on average 476 (Mean; range, 130-785) trials per session\" |\n   156→| Correct rate | 84 %, range 65–99 % | \"with 84% correct rate (range, 65-99%)\" |\n   157→| Session selection | performance > 65 %, ≥ 50 correct lick-left **and** ≥ 50 correct lick-right | STAR Methods |\n   158→| Photostim trials | ~25 % of trials, randomly interleaved | \"deployed on a subset of ~25% randomly interleaved trials\" |\n   159→| Photoinhibition effect | performance 83.2 % → 71.7 % (n = 17 mice, 93 sessions) | STAR Methods |\n   160→| Neural bin (method paper) | width 40 ms, stride 3.4 ms | \"we binned spikes into firing rates with a bin width of 40 ms and a stride of 3.4 ms\" |\n   161→| Video / behavior bin | 300 Hz, dt = 3.4 ms | \"high-speed (300 Hz) multiview video\"; `align_markers.py` `dt = 0.0034` |\n   162→| Task structure | sample (3 × 150 ms tones, 100 ms gaps ⇒ 0.65 s), delay 1.2 s, go cue 0.1 s, answer 1.5 s | methods.txt |\n   163→| Sessions used in method paper | 105–106 (those with usable video) | \"n = 105 sessions\", \"n = 106 sessions\" |\n   164→\n   165→### Processing Details\n   166→- **Temporal alignment**: everything is aligned to the **go cue**. The reference `.mat` export\n   167→  stores spike times already relative to the go cue; markers are aligned by subtracting\n   168→  `task_cue_time[0]` (= go cue) (`align_markers.py`).\n   169→- **Windows**: reference ephys window `[-3.0, 3.0]` s (script) / `[-3.0, 3.5]` s (module default)\n   170→  around the go cue; marker window `[-3, 1.5)` s.\n   171→- **Binning**: firing **rates** (Hz) = counts / bin width.\n   172→- **Markers**: outliers detected with a 5σ threshold on inter-frame velocity and imputed from\n   173→  neighbouring frames; \"when the tongue was occluded while it was in the mouth … we set the tongue\n   174→  position to its mean value\" — i.e. *tongue occlusion is explicitly a distinct state*, which is\n   175→  exactly the \"not visible\" class (3) required here.\n   176→\n   177→### Curation Steps\n   178→\n   179→**Neuron curation rules**\n   180→- Kilosort2 output → 15 quality metrics → five region-specific logistic-regression classifiers →\n   181→  label `good` / `unlabelled`. Only `good` units are analysed (⇒ `classification == 'good'`).\n   182→- Reference additionally requires a valid CCF annotation (equivalent in NWB, see Step 1).\n   183→- Method paper only: neurons with mean firing rate < 2 Hz excluded *from the firing-rate\n   184→  prediction (R²) analyses*; explicitly stated to be insensitive to the threshold.\n   185→- Recordings with substantial drift were rejected at the penetration level (QC white paper); the\n   186→  residue of this manual annotation is the per-insertion `is_good_trials` vector.\n   187→\n   188→**Trial curation rules**\n   189→- Data paper: \"Early lick trials and no response trials were excluded for analysis.\"\n   190→- Method paper: \"The dataset contains trials with photoinhibition, water administration regardless\n   191→  of the animals' choice (free water trials), early licks and trials where the animal ignores the\n   192→  lick-spouts. These were excluded from all analyses.\"\n   193→- Reference code `get_regular_trial_mask`: `early_lick == 0 & auto_water == 0 & free_water == 0 &\n   194→  correctness != -1 & stimulation == 0`.\n   195→\n   196→### Decoders Trained (accuracies reported in the papers)\n   197→| Decoded variable | Method | Accuracy |\n   198→|---|---|---|\n   199→| Choice (lick left/right), from **video** embedding, response epoch | logistic regression | ROC AUC 0.99 ± 0.01 (n = 106 sessions) |\n   200→| Choice from video, sample epoch (2nd half) | logistic regression | AUC ≈ 0.6 (highly variable across sessions) |\n   201→| Choice from video, pre-sample | — | AUC 0.51 ± 0.06 (chance) |\n   202→| Choice from video markers, response epoch | logistic regression | 0.88 ± 0.01 |\n   203→| Choice (lick left/right) from **neural** pseudo-populations (200 neurons/area) | logistic regression, nested 5-fold CV | ≈ 0.9–1.0 after the go cue; rises through sample/delay (data paper Fig 6D) |\n   204→| Single-neuron choice selectivity | d′ / AUC | AUC > 0.65 used as \"choice-modulated\" threshold |\n   205→\n   206→No paper reports decoding of `outcome`, `early lick` or `tongue position` from neural activity,\n   207→so there is no direct published accuracy target for three of the four outputs. Choice decoding\n   208→from a full simultaneously-recorded population after the go cue should be near-perfect\n   209→(≥ 0.9 balanced accuracy) if the conversion is correct.\n   210→\n   211→---\n   212→\n   213→## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n   214→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   215→\n   216→### Discrepancies Found\n   217→| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Papers say | Resolution |\n   218→|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n   219→| Number of sessions | — | 174 NWB files, **173** with ≥ 1 good unit | 173 behavioral sessions | The file `SC017_20190216_162508_s4` has 0 good units (4 insertions). Excluding it gives exactly 173 sessions **and** 659 − 4 = **655** insertions, matching the paper exactly. ⇒ drop that session. |\n   220→| Good units | — | 69,453 | 69,943 | 99.3 % agreement. The paper's number predates the DANDI release version 0.231012.2129 used here. Accepted, documented. |\n   221→| Trials/session | — | mean 545.9 raw; **483.8** excluding early-lick trials | mean 476 | The paper's \"trials per session\" evidently counts trials after removing early-lick trials (483.8 vs 476, 1.6 % apart). Consistent. |\n   222→| Correct rate | `correctness` 1/0/−1 | hit/(hit+miss) on non-early, non-photostim trials: mean **0.810**, range 0.54–0.97 (0.806 / max 0.989 if auto- and free-water trials are also removed) | 84 %, range 65–99 % | Same definition, 3 % lower. The upper end matches (0.989 ≈ 99 %). 16–23 sessions fall below the stated 65 % criterion. Since the *session* and *insertion* counts match the paper exactly, the DANDI release **is** the analysed set; the published selection criteria were applied at an earlier stage / on a slightly different definition. ⇒ **do not** re-apply a performance filter (it would remove sessions the authors kept). |\n   223→| Photostim fraction | `stimulation[:,0] != 0` | 18,588 / 94,990 = **19.6 %** of trials | \"~25 % randomly interleaved\" (17/25 mice) | Consistent: not every session/mouse had photostimulation, and the quoted 25 % is per photostim session. |\n   224→| Trial exclusions | exclude early-lick, no-response, photostim, auto-water, free-water | — | same | **Conflict with the decoder task**: `early lick` and `outcome (ignore)` are required decoder *outputs* and photostimulation is a required decoder *input*. These three exclusions are therefore dropped. Auto-water and free-water trials have no such requirement and are excluded, as in the reference. |\n   225→| `outcome` semantics | `correctness` 1 = correct, 0 = error, −1 = no response | verified against lick times: on `hit` trials the animal licks the instructed port (100 %), on `miss` trials it *first* licks the opposite port (100 %), on `ignore` trials there are no licks in [go, go+1.5] (100 %) | hit / miss / ignore | Confirmed; `choice` can be derived from (`trial_instruction`, `outcome`). |\n   226→| Observation window | reference truncates spikes to a fixed window around the go cue and bins the whole window | `obs_intervals` == trials `[start_time, stop_time]`; **no spikes exist outside them** | — | The per-trial recorded interval covers `go − 3.15 s` (median) to `go + 1.80 s` (median). 96.9 % of trials cover −2.5 s and 84.4 % cover +1.5 s. On error (`miss`) trials the interval ends at the error lick, so only 8 % of miss trials reach go+1.5 s. The reference does the same thing we will do — bin the full fixed window, which yields zeros where nothing was recorded. Documented as a known caveat (it makes `outcome` partly decodable from the zero-padding). |\n   227→| Tongue tracking availability | method paper used 105–106 sessions | `Camera0_side_TongueTracking` present in **all 174** sessions | \"105/106 sessions\" | The method paper additionally required a usable *raw video* (for the autoencoder/end-to-end pipelines) and `align_markers.py` skips sessions missing any of 8 markers (incl. whisker). DLC tongue markers are available everywhere, so no session needs to be dropped for the tongue output. |\n   228→\n   229→---\n   230→\n   231→## Step 5: Mapping Planning\n   232→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   233→\n   234→### Variable Mapping\n   235→| Source (NWB) | Target field | Transform | Reference analogue |\n   236→|---|---|---|---|\n   237→| `units.spike_times` (absolute) + `BehavioralEvents.go_start_times.timestamps` | `neural` | subtract go-cue time per trial; keep spikes in `[-2.5, 1.5)`; histogram into 80 non-overlapping 50 ms bins; divide by 0.05 ⇒ Hz | `process_one_area` + `sliding_histogram(rate=True)` |\n   238→| `units.classification == 'good'` | neuron filter | keep only `good` | `idx_qc_dict` (`qc_mode='classifier'`) |\n   239→| `units.anno_name` + electrode CCF `x` | `brain_regions`, `brain_region_idx` | map Allen annotation → one of 14 coarse regions; hemisphere from `x >= 5700` | `helper_get_neuron_id_area` |\n   240→| `BehavioralEvents.sample_start_times` (last one before the go cue) | `input[0]` `time_from_tone_onset_s` | `t_bin_center − t_tone`, seconds, continuous | `task_sample_time` |\n   241→| `BehavioralEvents.photostim_start_times` / `photostim_stop_times` | `input[1]` `photostim_on` | 1 if the photostim interval overlaps the bin, else 0 | `task_stimulation` cols 2–3 |\n   242→| `trials.trial_instruction` + `trials.outcome` | `output[0]` `lick_direction_choice` | hit ⇒ instructed side; miss ⇒ opposite side; ignore ⇒ no lick. 0 = left, 1 = right, 2 = no lick | `lick_directions` / `correctness` |\n   243→| `trials.outcome` | `output[1]` `outcome` | 0 = ignore, 1 = miss, 2 = hit | `correctness` (−1/0/1) |\n   244→| `trials.early_lick` | `output[2]` `early_lick` | 0 = no, 1 = yes | `behavior_early_report` |\n   245→| `BehavioralTimeSeries.Camera0_side_TongueTracking` (`y`, `likelihood`) | `output[3]` `tongue_y_position` | per 50 ms bin: mean `y` over frames of *that trial* with `likelihood > 0.9`; if no such frame ⇒ 3 (not visible); else 0/1/2 by session percentiles (<40th, 40–60th, >60th) | `tracking.camera_0_side.tongue_y` |\n   246→| `nwb.subject.subject_id` | `subjects`, `subject_idx` | unique sorted list | — |\n   247→| `trials.auto_water`, `trials.free_water` | trial filter | drop | `get_regular_trial_mask` |\n   248→| `units.is_good_trials` | trial filter | drop trials flagged `False` for any retained unit | drift QC (white paper) |\n   249→\n   250→### Key Decisions\n   251→1. **Alignment = go cue** (`BehavioralEvents['go_start_times'].timestamps`, one per trial).\n   252→   Matches both papers and all reference code.\n   253→2. **Window = [−2.5, +1.5] s, 80 non-overlapping 50 ms bins** (task spec). Bin *k* covers\n   254→   `[-2.5 + 0.05k, -2.5 + 0.05(k+1))`, centre `-2.475 + 0.05k`.\n   255→3. **Firing rates in Hz** (counts / 0.05 s), as `sliding_histogram(rate=True)`.\n   256→4. **Neuron filter: `classification == 'good'` only.** This is exactly the classifier-based QC of\n   257→   the data paper. The method paper's extra \"≥ 2 Hz\" filter is *not* applied: it was introduced to\n   258→   stabilise per-neuron R² estimates in the video→firing-rate regression, not for decoding, and\n   259→   discarding low-rate neurons would throw away decodable information.\n   260→5. **Trial filter**: drop `auto_water` and `free_water` trials (reference); drop trials not\n   261→   flagged good in `is_good_trials` for any retained unit (drift QC). **Keep** early-lick,\n   262→   `ignore`, and photostim trials — required by the decoder input/output spec.\n   263→6. **Session filter**: drop sessions with 0 good units (1 session) and sessions with < 2 trials\n   264→   after curation. No behavioural-performance filter (see Step 4).\n   265→7. **Zero padding outside the recorded interval** is kept rather than dropping trials, matching\n   266→   `process_one_area`, because dropping would remove ~92 % of `miss` trials and destroy the\n   267→   `outcome` output. Documented caveat.\n   268→8. **Tone onset = last `sample_start_time` before the go cue** — with early licks the sample epoch\n   269→   is replayed, and the last replay is the instruction that actually precedes the delay/go.\n   270→   Median tone→go = 1.85 s (= 0.65 s sample + 1.2 s delay), as expected from the task design.\n   271→9. **`time_from_tone_onset` is a continuous ramp** (explicitly required by the task spec:\n   272→   \"continuous, time-varying\"), not a binary indicator.\n   273→10. **Tongue visibility threshold: DLC likelihood > 0.9.** The likelihood distribution is\n   274→    strongly bimodal (≈ 3–6 × 10⁻⁵ vs ≈ 1.0); any threshold in [0.1, 0.99] gives the same answer\n   275→    to within 0.1 % of frames. ~6–11 % of frames have the tongue visible.\n   276→11. **Tongue percentiles are computed per session** over the *binned, visible* values of all\n   277→    retained trials of that session (the quantity actually being discretised).\n   278→12. **Per-trial outputs are broadcast across time**, because the output array must have a single\n   279→    shape and the tongue output is time-varying.\n   280→13. **Brain regions: 14 coarse areas** (the reference's list). ALM is defined as units on\n   281→    ALM-targeted insertions annotated to secondary motor / primary motor / frontal-pole areas.\n   282→    Hemisphere is stored separately in metadata rather than doubling the region list.\n   283→\n   284→### Planned Sanity Checks\n   285→- [ ] 173 sessions, 28 subjects, 655 insertions, ≈ 69.4 k good units.\n   286→- [ ] Trials/session (excluding early licks) ≈ 476.\n   287→- [ ] Photostim trials ≈ 20 % of trials; photostim always ends before the go cue and lies in the\n   288→      delay epoch.\n   289→- [ ] Median tone→go interval = 1.85 s.\n   290→- [ ] `choice` derived from (instruction, outcome) agrees with the direction of the **first lick**\n   291→      after the go cue on ≥ 99 % of hit/miss trials, and `ignore` trials have no licks.\n   292→- [ ] Tongue visible ≈ 6–11 % of frames; visible bins concentrated after the go cue.\n   293→- [ ] Tongue class fractions ≈ 40 / 20 / 40 of the *visible* bins.\n   294→- [ ] Per-neuron mean firing rate in the converted data matches `units.avg_firing_rate` in order\n   295→      of magnitude, and a direct spike-count recomputation from the raw NWB matches exactly.\n   296→- [ ] Neural, input and output spot-checks against the raw NWB with `np.allclose`.\n   297→\n   298→---\n   299→\n   300→## Step 6: Script Development\n   301→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   302→\n   303→`/app/convert_data.py` (+ `/app/ccf_regions.py` for the CCF annotation → coarse-area map).\n   304→\n   305→Structure:\n   306→- `list_session_files()` — enumerate the 174 NWB files.\n   307→- `process_session(path)` — everything for one session; returns `None` if the session is dropped.\n   308→- `bin_spikes_rate(spike_times, go_times)` — vectorised binning. The per-trial bin edges are\n   309→  built as `go[:,None] + BIN_EDGES[None,:]` and located in the (sorted) spike train with a single\n   310→  `np.searchsorted`, so there is no Python loop over trials; the counts are `np.diff` of the\n   311→  resulting indices, divided by the bin width to give Hz.\n   312→- `interval_overlap_bins()` — photostimulation indicator.\n   313→- `bin_tongue()` — assigns every video frame to its trial, keeps visible frames\n   314→  (likelihood > 0.9) inside the window, and averages `y` per (trial, bin) with two\n   315→  `np.bincount` calls.\n   316→- `discretise_tongue()` — 40th/60th session percentiles over the visible bins.\n   317→- `plot_processing()` — the `--show-processing` figure (8 panels, one per processing step).\n   318→- `main()` — `multiprocessing.Pool` over sessions (default 16 workers), assembles the final dict.\n   319→\n   320→Bugs found and fixed while developing:\n   321→1. **`is_good_trials` / `obs_intervals` are not always `n_trials` long.** In 9 sessions the ephys\n   322→   recording covers only a subset of the behavioural trials (e.g. `SC015_20190208_133600_s2`:\n   323→   160 of 480 trials). `units.obs_intervals` is identical for every unit of a session and its\n   324→   rows match the `[start_time, stop_time]` of the *observed* trials exactly (checked for all\n   325→   174 files). The script now locates the observed trials in the trials table by `start_time`\n   326→   and restricts everything to them. Note 8 of the 9 are a prefix of the trials table but\n   327→   `SC026_20190807_134913_s20` is not, so a simple \"first N trials\" rule would have been wrong.\n   328→2. **Trials with literally no spikes.** The recording can stop part-way through the last\n   329→   annotated trial, leaving a trial in which not one of hundreds of good units fires. Such\n   330→   trials are dropped (1 trial in the 2-session sample; they also triggered an\n   331→   \"all neural data is zero\" warning from the verifier).\n   332→\n   333→Code inefficiencies identified / speedups added:\n   334→- Reading spike times per unit through `units['spike_times'][i]` is slow; the whole flattened\n   335→  ragged dataset is read once (`sv.target.data[:]`, ~90 MB per session, 0.13 s) and sliced.\n   336→- Per-trial Python loops for binning replaced by a single vectorised `np.searchsorted`.\n   337→- Tongue binning done with `np.bincount` instead of a per-bin loop.\n   338→- Sessions processed in parallel with `multiprocessing` (16 workers).\n   339→\n   340→---\n   341→\n   342→## Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n   343→**Status**: COMPLETE\n
